# Seed 54 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - small (Iteration 333)

`seed 54 tag2 9h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 6 --frontier --cpus 2,8`, commit
1ba89294d (Iteration 321) - the second run of the after-seed-52 genes (the JIT fix, bfind). The
chain 03:15-11:55 UTC Oct 16 (8 h 40 min): the evolution 03:25-10:12 (6 h 47 min), the re-measure
24 min, the comparison at 6 rounds 1 h 18 min. 41,406 designs: 31,219 measured, 10,010 screened.

The comparison's verdict (five measures, 6 rounds): fastest +3.6%, smallest +0.05%, memory -2 KB,
hypervolume +1.1%, best Q +0.9% - **small**; with seed 53's, two small in a row.

- **bfind on 67 of the 195 designs of the front** (17 of 172 in seed 53) - kept by seven hours of
  selection on the laptop's predictor, which the VM's noise could not show.
- No corpus deaths. One new death: a2c8d50d0a, "image did not convert (AssertionError: call target
  1390 not 8-aligned)" - the converter refusing its own output for one combination of genes, one of
  41,406 (results/drafts/convert-align-death.md).
- Children: mutation 54.3%, crossover 34.4% (the last fifth 36.7%), borrowed 11.3%; the JIT on 133.
- **dream.lan** (results/xcheck-dream-seed54.md): 24 designs of seed 53's front - Spearman 0.941,
  the laptop's six fastest ranked 3 4 2 10 1 5 there. rage.lan: failed - the old code (the fix is
  the pending after-seed-54 batch).
- Held out (heldout.py):
  sieve (held out): 195 designs; Spearman with the selection speed 0.953
    the 10 fastest by selection, their rank on it: 5 6 2 27 11 8 9 7 3 1
    held-out / selection speed: min 0.36, median 0.54, max 2.73
    flattered most by the selection: 3183f80357 2.73, 2196fb7d94 2.63, 1e3d59d067 2.56, b3bae05abe 2.52, 598f696e62 2.46

---

# Evolved VM designs

31397 designs evaluated, 31219 alive, 10010 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): d02f16abd1, speed 0.146, binary + image 28,009, memory 160 KB - Q 0.200 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
